    local function func78()
        local Plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

        if not Plots then
            return nil
        end

        local LocalPlayerName = LocalPlayer.Name
        local DisplayName = LocalPlayer.DisplayName

        for _, v in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
            local PlotSign = v:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local Text = nil

            if PlotSign then
                for _, v2 in ipairs(PlotSign:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v2:IsA("TextLabel") and v2.Text and #v2.Text > 0 then
                        Text = v2.Text
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if not Text or not Text:find(DisplayName, 1, true) and not Text:find(LocalPlayerName, 1, true) then
                continue
            end

            local TreadmillBottom = v:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom")

            if TreadmillBottom and TreadmillBottom:IsA("BasePart") then
                return TreadmillBottom
            end
        end

        return nil
    end

    function tbl24.hlHideOne(arg1)
        local HL = tbl24.HL

        if arg1:IsA("BasePart") then
            arg1.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
            HL.touched[arg1] = true

            return
        end

        if arg1:IsA("Decal") or arg1:IsA("Texture") then
            if HL.touched[arg1] == nil then
                HL.touched[arg1] = arg1.Transparency
            end

            arg1.Transparency = 1

            return
        end

        if arg1:IsA("BillboardGui") or arg1:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
            if arg1.Enabled then
                HL.touched[arg1] = "gui"
                arg1.Enabled = false
            end
        elseif (arg1:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or arg1:IsA("Trail") or arg1:IsA("PointLight") or arg1:IsA("SurfaceLight") or arg1:IsA("SpotLight")) and arg1.Enabled then
            HL.touched[arg1] = "fx"
            arg1.Enabled = false
        end
    end

    function tbl24.hnApply(instance)

                pcall(function()

                end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function func105()
        local Plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

        if not Plots then
            return nil
        end

        local LocalPlayerName = LocalPlayer.Name
        local DisplayName = LocalPlayer.DisplayName

        for _, v in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
            local PlotSign = v:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local Text = nil

            if PlotSign then
                for _, v2 in ipairs(PlotSign:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v2:IsA("TextLabel") and v2.Text and #v2.Text > 0 then
                        Text = v2.Text
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if Text and (Text:find(DisplayName, 1, true) or Text:find(LocalPlayerName, 1, true)) then
                return v
            end
        end

        return nil
    end

                local tbl32 = func122()

                if not next(tbl32) then
                    local tr = (getgenv().__SAE_LANG or {}).tr or function(arg2)
                        return arg2
                    end

                    local var48 = tr("Index: ") .. (tbl19[flags.indexArea] and tbl19[flags.indexArea].display or "stage") .. tr(" complete!")

                    if var17 then
                        pcall(function()
                            var17:Notify(var48, 2.2)
                        end)
                    end

                    flags.autoIndex = false
                    break
                end

                local var48 = nil

                if var33 then
                    pcall(function()
                        var48 = var33.Get()
                    end)
                end

                local var49 = if type(var48) == "table" then var48 or nil else nil
                local num22 = 0

                if var49 and type(var49.EggInventory) == "table" then
                    for k, v in pairs(var49.EggInventory) do
                        if v.AssetCategory and tbl32[v.AssetCategory] and not func107(k) then
                            num22 += 1
                        end
                    end
                end

                local num23 = #func119()
                tbl23.index = num22 > 0

                if num22 == 0 and num23 == 0 then
                    local var50 = ((getgenv().__SAE_LANG or {}).tr or function(arg2)
                        return arg2
                    end)("Auto Index: no eggs left for this stage — stopped")

                    if var17 then
                        pcall(function()
                            var17:Notify(var50, 2.2)
                        end)
                    end

                    flags.autoIndex = false
                    break
                end

                local bool9 = false
                local bool10 = false

                if num22 > 0 then
                    gotoPen()

                    if var49 and type(var49.EggInventory) == "table" then
                        for k, v in pairs(var49.EggInventory) do
                            if not flags.autoIndex then
                                break
                            end

                            if not v.AssetCategory or not tbl32[v.AssetCategory] then
                                continue
                            end

                            local var50 = placeOneEgg(k)

                            if var50 == true then
                                task.wait(0.3)
                                bool9 = true
                            elseif var50 ~= "skip" then
                                if var50 == "far" then
                                    gotoPen()
                                    bool10 = true
                                end

                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not (bool9 or bool10) then
                    __SAEGlide.release("place")
                end

                pcall(func120)
                task.wait(bool9 and 2 or 5)
            end

            __SAEGlide.release("place")
            tbl23.index = false
        end)
    end

    local tbl32 = {}
    local var48 = Color3.fromRGB(61, 220, 151)
    local var49 = Color3.fromRGB(96, 112, 128)

    local function func124(arg1)
        local var50 = tbl19[arg1]
        local result = {}

        if var50 then
            local var51 = nil

            if var33 then
                pcall(function()
                    var51 = var33.Get()
                end)
            end

            local var52 = if type(var51) == "table" then var51 or nil else nil
            local var53 = if var52 and type(var52.Index) == "table" then var52.Index or {} else {}

            for _, v in ipairs(var50.cats) do
                local var54 = tbl5[v]
                local bool9 = var53[v] == true
                local num22 = #result + 1
                local tbl33 = {
                    value = v
                }
                local var55 = if var54 then var54.display or v else v
                tbl33.name = var55 .. (bool9 and "  ✓" or "")
                tbl33.icon = var54 and var54.icon
                tbl33.badge = bool9 and var48 or var49

                function tbl33.tip()
                    return var54 and var54.display or v, {
                        {
                            text = bool9 and "Collected" or "Missing",
                            color = bool9 and var48 or var49
                        }
                    }
                end

                result[num22] = tbl33
            end
        end

        return result
    end

            end)

            return tostring(var52 or ""):lower():sub(1, 2) == "th" and "th" or "en"
        end

                return true

        end

        end

        function SuijFeatures.tierId()
            local tier = SUJI and SUJI.tier or "free"
            local var51 = tostring(tier):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("%+", "plus")

            return if var51:find("plus", 1, true) then "premplus" elseif var51:find("prem", 1, true) then "prem" else "free"
        end

        function SuijFeatures.tierName()
            return tbl34[SuijFeatures.tierId()] or "Free"
        end

        function SuijFeatures.needTier(arg1)
            local featureTier = SUJI and SUJI.featureTiers and SUJI.featureTiers[arg1]

            if featureTier then
                return tbl34[tostring(featureTier):lower()] or tostring(featureTier)

        end

        end

                    arg1 = var53

                end

                var53 = tonumber(arg1.Premium)

                        premium = arg1["premium+"]
                        var53 = tonumber(premium)

                        if var53 then

        end

        function SuijFeatures.planLine()
            local var51 = SUJI

            if var51 then
                var51 = SUJI.exp or SUJI.expiresAt or SUJI.expiry
                var51 = var51 or SUJI.expireAt or SUJI.expires
            end

            local var52 = tonumber(var51)

            if var52 and var52 > 0 then
                if var52 > 1000000000000 then
                    var52 /= 1000
                end

                local var53, var54 = pcall(function()
                    return os.date("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M", var52)
                end)

                if var53 then
                    return SuijFeatures.tierName() .. " · " .. var54
                end
            end

            return SuijFeatures.tierName()
        end

        function SuijFeatures.planChip()
            local var51 = SUJI

            if var51 then
                var51 = SUJI.exp
                var51 = var51 or SUJI.expiresAt or SUJI.expiry
            end

            local var52 = tonumber(var51) or 0

            if var52 > 1000000000000 then
                var52 /= 1000
            end

            local var53 = var52 - os.time()

            if not (var52 > 0 and var53 > 0) then
                return SuijFeatures.tierName()
            end

            local var54 = math.floor(var53 / 86400)
            local var55 = math.floor(var53 % 86400 / 3600)
            local var56

            if var54 > 0 then
                var56 = var54 .. "d " .. var55 .. "h"
            elseif var55 > 0 then
                var56 = var55 .. "h " .. math.floor(var53 % 3600 / 60) .. "m"
            else
                var56 = math.max(1, math.floor(var53 / 60)) .. "m"
            end

            return SuijFeatures.tierName() .. " · " .. var56
        end
    end

    function SuijFeatures.get(arg1)
        return flags[arg1]
    end

    function SuijFeatures.set(arg1, arg2)
        if var20[arg1] == nil then
            return false
        end

        flags[arg1] = arg2

        if var17 and var17.onChanged then
            pcall(var17.onChanged, arg1, arg2)
        end

        return true
    end

    function SuijFeatures.list()
        local result = {}

        for k in pairs(var20) do
            result[k] = flags[k]
        end

        return result
    end

    function SuijFeatures.ids()
        local result = {}

        for k in pairs(var20) do
            result[#result + 1] = k
        end

        table.sort(result)

        return result
    end
